# =============================================================================
# .gitignore  ·  通用安全全集模板
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 用途：任何新项目开 git 之前，把这个文件复制成项目根目录的 .gitignore
# 结构：三层防护 —— 🔑 密钥本体 / 🚪 网关令牌 / 🔐 夹带密钥的产物
# 原则：宁可多挡，不可漏挡。被多挡的文件不会丢，只是不进仓库。
#
# 用法：整段复制到项目根目录，命名为 .gitignore（前面有个点，是隐藏文件）
# 维护：项目里用不到的整段（比如 Java 段、.NET 段）可以整段删掉，不影响其他
# =============================================================================


# #############################################################################
# 🔑 第一层：密钥本体 —— API Key / 令牌 / 密码 / 私钥
# #############################################################################
# 这一层是底线。只要命中这里，git 根本不会把文件纳入版本控制，
# 也就谈不上"不小心提交"。

# --- .env 全家族 -------------------------------------------------------------
# .env / .env.local / .env.production / .env.dev.local ……
# 注意：单独放行 .env.example 作为公开模板，但那个文件里只能有假值。
.env
.env.*
!.env.example
!.env.template
!.env.sample

# --- 本地覆盖配置（个人机器专用，绝不进仓库）---------------------------------
config.local.*

So rerun in UI is user-driven: accept revisions, click rerun. In replay mode, replay client looks up cassette by digest. If prompt doesn't match a cassette, it throws cassette-missing. So for the demo to demonstrate rerun, the cassettes for the second run need to exist — which requires the second run to use exactly the same prompts as the user's second run. The revised resume text must match exactly what the user would produce by clicking "全部采纳" on the demo session.

Goal: offline demo (no API key) should also demonstrate "改完再跑一遍 → 比上次". Currently seed generates 9 combos of cassettes for the first run only. Now I understand the setup. Let me think about the C1 offline demo second run.

Goal: offline demo should demonstrate "改完再跑一遍" — i.e., seed should create a second session whose resume is the accepted-revised text of session 1, with parentSessionId pointing to session 1, so opening it in the UI shows the compare bar with improved/worsened.

Considerations:
- The second run's prompts differ (resume text changed), so new cassettes.
- The script must in the second run produce claims from the NEW resume text. But wait: extract returns fixed DEMO_PLANS claims with fixed quotes. After revision, the quotes may no longer exist in the revised resume → anchoring rejects them → claims dropped. That's a big problem.

Options:

The script's extract returns quotes; C2's quote won't be found → dropped → 5 claims become 4... Also C3, C4, C5 have suggestions too. If we accept all 4, the resume changes a lot.

Managing the scripted demo to produce a meaningful second run is a big design lift: the script would need per-variant content (a "second-run plan" where quotes match the revised text and verdicts are better).

Alternative cheaper approach: **make the demo second run use only a subset** — accept just C2 (the overstated one) whose revised text is anchored. Then the second run's resume contains the revised C2 sentence. Script for second session: a separate DEMO_AFTER plan list with quotes taken from the revised text (for default style normal), verdicts: C2 solid now (evidence available), others same.

But the script is style-dependent (afterByStyle), and cassettes exist for 9 combos. Two-run demo per combo would multiply cassettes: 9 combos × 2 runs. And second-run prompts depend on revised text which depends on style. That's ~2× cassettes. Currently 56; maybe ~112.
